Objective in normalized sum S=1: minimize M=max c_k. Since n up to few thousands, c_k vector length 2n-1. c = conv(a,a). We can optimize over a≥0, sum=1. This is a nonconvex high-dimensional problem. We can use subgradient descent with smoothing (like previous). Need improve. Potential strategies:

1. Use better normalization and parameterization: a = softmax(w) / sum? Or a_i = exp(u_i), normalized by sum. This maintains positivity and deals with scale. But endpoints may need zero; can use positive with large range. Gradient of log? Maybe.

2. Use dual formulation: For fixed M, minimize? We can use subgradient ascent on Lagrange multiplier for target? Since objective = [2n/S^2] * max c. Set S=1. Minimize max c. Equivalent to find max λ such that constraints c_k ≤M. Subgradient w.r.t a of c_k is (2 correlate). Using top K constraints active set.

3. Use spectral/FFT for fast updates. Compute c via FFT; soft gradient via inverse FFT of weights? Let's derive efficient gradient.

Given a length n, define b= np.pad(a, (0,n-1))? Convolution c = a * a, length 2n-1. Softmax weights p over c, objective smooth L=∑ p_k c_k (if p softmax with tau). Pseudo for `_run_opt(a,n,deadline,tag, use_smooth, K, eta0, tau0, tau_end, momentum)`:
- a = project(a,n); best=a,best_o=obj.
- v = zeros.
- beta=0.9 maybe, decay.
- stall counter.
- loop until deadline:
   c=conv(a,a); M=max(c); o=2M/n.
   update best if o<best_o-1e-10.
   Determine weights:
      If use_smooth: p=softmax((c - M)/tau) maybe centered; or p=softmax(c/tau).
      If hard top: p=zeros; idx=topk(c, K) or active within eps; p[idx]=1/len.
   g=2*correlate(p,a,'valid')
   g -= g.mean()
   Normalize: gm = max(abs(g)); if gm small break.
   Momentum: v = beta*v + (1-beta)*g (or after normalize). Use normalize before momentum? Maybe v=beta*v+(1-beta)*g; gm2=max(abs(v)); direction=v/gm2.
   Candidate step: step_size = eta (maybe 0.02-0.05). a_new=project(a - step_size*direction, n)
   Maybe try multiple step sizes: For high-res, evaluating 2 extra candidates per iteration too expensive? 0.1s each, can do 3 per it -> 0.3s, still ~8000 steps. Good. Use line search: candidates step = [eta, 0.5eta, 2eta, maybe eta*random]. Compute objective for best 2? We can compute c once for candidate. But direction may not improve every time due to projection. We can maintain eta adaptively: if improvement, eta*=1.05; else eta*=0.8.
   Simpler: evaluate a_new. If o_new < o - tol, update a, eta=min(eta*1.02, max_eta); else eta*=0.9 and maybe try smaller if very bad. This is like backtracking without extra evals except one. But if current step bad, we still evaluate one. Good.
   If no improvement for many iters, reduce eta, change K/tau, or perturbation.

We can use FFT for full objective and gradient to allow more steps/line search. Let's investigate FFT implementation.

Convolution via FFT:
```
def _fft_conv(a):
    n=len(a); L=1
    need=2*n-1
    while L<need: L*=2
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
    fc=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, L)[:need]
    return np.real(fc)
```
For n=3809, L=8192, FFT size 8192. Much faster than direct O(n^2) maybe. But numerical errors? Double, fine. Gradient cross-correlation via FFT:
Need g[j] = 2∑_{r=0}^{n-1} a[r] p[j+r], p length Lp=2n-1. This is cross-correlation of p and a. We can compute by zero-padding both to L >= len(p)+len(a)-1? Let's derive using FFT.
We want for j=0..n-1: ∑_{r=0}^{n-1} a_r p_{r+j}. Define p_pad length L, a_pad length L. Cross-correlation maybe `irfft(fft(p) * conj(fft(a_rev)))`? Easier use direct for high-res? FFT gradient: let a_rev = np.flip(a). The sum ∑ a_r p_{r+j} is convolution of p with a_rev? (p * a_rev)[j+n-1] = ∑_i p_i a_rev[j+n-1-i] = ∑_i p_i a_{n-1-(j+n-1-i)}=∑_i p_i a_{i-j}. Not. Let's derive.
We want h[j]=∑_{r} a_r p_{r+j}. This is cross-correlation of p (long) and a: correlate(p,a)[j] = ∑_r p_{r+j} a_r. In numpy, correlate(p,a). Via FFT, correlate(x,y) = ifft(fft(y) * conj(fft(x))) with appropriate padding? Standard cross-correlation: (x ⋆ y)[k] = ∑ x[n+k] y[n]. This is correlation of x with y. If x=p, y=a, output length len(p)-len(a)+1 for valid. Let L>=len(p)+len(a)-1. Let X=fft(x,L), Y=fft(y,L). Then `irfft(X * conj(Y))`? Check simple: x=[x0,x1], y=[y0,y1]. correlation valid? Full correlation c[k]=∑ x[n+k] y[n] for k negative? Actually standard `np.correlate(x,y,'full')` output length N+M-1: c[m] = ∑_n x[n+m] y[n] for m? Need not. We can avoid FFT gradient by using direct if p dense? For n=3809 direct correlate length 7617 and 3809 ~? 28M? okay. FFT overhead maybe but we can derive and implement if needed.

Let's derive using convolution. h[j]=∑_{r=0}^{n-1} a_r p_{r+j}. Let p reversed? Define q = reverse(p) of length 2n-1: q[i]=p[2n-2-i]. Then convolution q * a: (q*a)[t]=∑_{r} q[t-r] a_r = ∑_r p[2n-2 - (t-r)] a_r = ∑_r p[2n-2 - t + r] a_r. Set t = 2n-2 - j => index =2n-2 - (2n-2 -j)+r = j+r. So h[j] = (q*a)[2n-2 - j]. Great.        # maybe also try full random init every 10 attempts using low-res best

    best_a,best_o = _run_opt(best_a,n, deadline-2, 'final', hard_only=True)
    # Finally, evaluate and return; if invalid fallback uniform or best.

Now code details.

Implement `_safe_obj_general(a)`:
```
def _safe_obj_general(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    if a.size==0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(a)): return np.inf
    a=np.maximum(a,0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<1e-8: return np.inf
    n=a.size
    c=np.convolve(a,a)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)): return np.inf
    return 2.0*n*float(np.max(c))/(s*s)
```
This can be used for candidates. For optimized sum=n, `_obj_n` faster uses max(c)/n*2.

`_full_conv(a)`: maybe use np.convolve; for large n>10000, maybe too slow. We'll limit n <=8192. If resample N2 maybe 4096. Okay.

`_project_n`:
```
def _project_n(a,n):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=1e-12:
        return np.full(n,1.0)
    a=a/s*n
    return a
```

`_softmax(c, tau)`: To avoid overflow, shift by max c. If tau <=1e-12, approximate top one equal among max? Use:
```
if tau <= 1e-12: p=zeros; idx=(c==max); p[idx]=1/count
else: e=exp((c-M)/tau); s=e.sum(); if s==0 return one hot; else p=e/s
```
Since c length 2n-1, memory okay.

`_weighted_gradient`: if mode top, use `_grad_hard_multi`; else `_grad_smooth`.

`_grad_smooth(p,a)`: We can use `np.correlate(p, a, mode='valid')`. Need ensure p length =2a-1. If p length different due to numerical? Fix by resizing? In code, p from c length 2n-1. Good. For n=1, valid length 1. Fine.

js=max(0,k-n+1); je=min(n-1,k)+1
if js<je:

    g[js:je] += 2.0*a[ia]

M=c.max()
if tol_frac>0:

    idx=np.where(c>=thresh)[0]

# if idx size > Kmax, top Kmax by c

```

def _low_res_search(target_n, init_a, rng, budget_s, deadline, use_parent=True):
    m = target_n
    while m > 256 and m > target_n//8: m//=2? Let's choose m = max(128, min(512, target_n//8)) maybe 512 for 3809.
    inits = []
    # downsample init to m if init length != m
    if init_a is not None: inits.append(_downsample(init_a,m))
    inits.append(np.ones(m))
    x=np.arange(m)
    inits.append(_project_n(x+1,m)) # ramp
    inits.append(_project_n(m-x+1,m))
    inits.append(_project_n(rng.dirichlet(alpha=0.5, size=m),m))
    # periodic high-low
    p=np.zeros(m); p[np.arange(0,m,2)]=1; inits.append(_project_n(p+0.2,m))
    # sine
    for s in [2,5]: p=1+0.5*np.sin(2*np.pi*s*x/m); inits.append(_project_n(p,m))
    # choose unique
    best=None
    sub_budget = max(5, (deadline - now - 10)/len(inits))
    for idx, a0 in enumerate(inits):
       rem_deadline=...
       a1,o1 = _run_opt(a0,m, min(time+sub_budget, deadline-5), f'low{idx}', mode='auto', eta0=0.06, ...)
       if best is None or o1<best_o: best...
    return best
```

def _downsample(a,N):

```
def _perturb_block(a, rng, sigma, width_frac):
    n=len(a); b=a.copy()
    w=max(2,int(n*width_frac))
    s=int(rng.integers(0,n-w+1))
    b[s:s+w]=b[s:s+w]*np.exp(rng.normal(0,sigma,size=w))
    return _project_n(b,n)
```

Maybe start beta=0.9 and decay to 0.5. Implement `momentum` parameter constant. If momentum internal, v initialized zeros. If a reset, reset v.

def _lp_refine(a,n,deadline,tag,max_iters=100):
    a=_project_n(a,n)
    best=a.copy(); best_o=_obj_n(a,n)
    eta? t=0.5
    it=0
    while time.monotonic()<deadline and it<max_iters:
       # ensure positive
       a=np.maximum(a,1e-6)
       s=a.sum(); a=a/n*s? Actually normalize sum=n: a = a/a.sum()*n
       c=np.convolve(a,a); M=float(c.max()); o=2*M/n
       if o<best_o: update
       # build C
       C=np.zeros((2*n-1,n))
       # for k in range(2*n-1): ...
       # Could use scipy?? No.
       # solve
       if linprog is None: break
       c_obj = -np.ones(n)
       b_vec = M*np.ones(2*n-1)
       minf = float(np.min(a))
       ub = max(1e6, M/(minf+1e-12))*np.ones(n)
       res=linprog(c_obj, A_ub=C, b_ub=b_vec, bounds=[(0,ub[i]) for i in range(n)], method='highs', options={'disp':False})
       if res.status not in (0,? maybe 2? accept if x finite)
       g=res.x
       # fallback if fail
       gsum=float(g.sum())
       if gsum <=0: continue
       g = g/gsum*n
       g=np.maximum(g,0)
       # line search t
       best_t=a; best_to=o
       for t in (0.3,0.5,0.8,1.0):
           cand=(1-t)*a + t*g
           cand=_project_n(cand,n)
           co=_obj_n(cand,n)
           if co<best_to: best=t; best_to=co
       a=best_t
       if a.sum()!=n project
       if best_to < best_o: best_o=best_to; best=a.copy()
       it++
       # if LP taking too long, break? time check
    return best,best_o
```

`_select_indices` code:
```
def _select_indices(c,n,Kmax):
    m=len(c)
    L=m-1
    M=float(c.max())
    tol=max(1e-12*M, 1e-12)
    # includes all near max; if none due to tol? at least max
    order=np.argsort(c)[::-1]
    seen=np.zeros(m,dtype=bool)
    out=[]
    for k_int in order.tolist():
        k=int(k_int)
        pk=L-k
        if seen[k] or seen[pk]:
            continue
        if c[k] < M - 0.25*M and len(out) >= 2: # only allow far peaks if very close? Maybe use threshold 0.05M.
            # continue or break? If top peaks only one pair, still can include lower if Kmax large. We want top K by value.
            pass
        out.append(k); out.append(pk)
        seen[k]=True; seen[pk]=True
        if len(out) >= Kmax:
            break
    if not out:
        out=[int(np.argmax(c))]
    return np.array(out, dtype=int)
```
This may include peaks even far if Kmax large; okay. But if one top pair and Kmax=20, it will add next highest pair maybe much lower; gradient diluted. Could use threshold: only include pairs with c[k] >= M - active_tol where active_tol = max(0.05*M, 0.01*n?) But if Kmax small, top pair only. Better:
```
active_tol = max(1e-9*M, 0.03*M)  # 3%
while out len < Kmax:
   if next c[k] >= M - active_tol: add pair
   else: if out len < 2: add pair (the top); else break
```

def _low_res_search(target_n, seed_a, rng, deadline, budget_s, use_lp=True):

Now, `propose_candidate` steps in detail:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    start=time.monotonic()
    deadline=start+max(60,float(budget_s))-5.0
    # get parent
    parent=None; n=3809
    try:
       par=height_sequence_1
       par=np.asarray(par,dtype=float)
       if par.ndim==1 and par.size>10 and np.all(np.isfinite(par)):
          parent=np.clip(par,0,1000)
          if parent.sum()<1e-6: parent=np.ones_like(parent)
          n=int(parent.size)
    except Exception: parent=None
    if parent is None:
       a=np.ones(n)
    else: a=parent
    a=_project_n(a,n)
    # quick variants list
    variants = _make_variants(a,n,rng) # top-level
    best_a=a.copy(); best_o=_obj_n(a,n)
    for v in variants:
       o=_obj_n(_project_n(v,n),n)
       if o<best_o: best_o=o; best_a=v.copy()
    print(initial)
    # if no parent and best_o >1.6 maybe initial low-res longer
    # Ensure at least some runtime for main
    main_deadline = min(start+180, deadline-30)
    best_a,best_o = _run_opt(best_a,n,main_deadline,'main_hard',mode='hard',eta0=0.025,Kmax=6,momentum=0.5,rng=rng)
    # Low-res if objective not close and time
    if best_o > 1.52 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 250:
       low_budget = min(120.0, max(60.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 400.0))
       if low_budget > 30:
          lp=True if linprog is not None else False
          low_a = _low_res_search(n, best_a, rng, min(deadline, time+low_budget), low_budget, use_lp=lp)
          if low_a is not None:
             up = _resample(low_a, n)
             o_up = _obj_n(up,n)
             if o_up < best_o + 0.05: # run on it
                sub = min(time+120, deadline-120)
                up,o_up2 = _run_opt(up,n,sub,'fromlow',mode='auto',eta0=0.04,Kmax=8,momentum=0.7,rng=rng)
                if o_up2<best_o: best_o=o_up2; best_a=up.copy()
                print
    # Maybe try larger length
    if best_o > 1.503 and n < 8192 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 350:
       n2=1
       while n2 < n: n2*=2
       if n2 == n: n2 = min(8192, n*2)
       if n2 <= 10000:
          a2=_resample(best_a,n2)
          o2=_obj_n(a2,n2)
          if o2 < best_o + 0.1:
             sub=min(time+180, deadline-180)
             a2,o2=_run_opt(a2,n2,sub,'bigger',mode='auto',eta0=0.04,Kmax=8,momentum=0.7,rng=rng)
             if o2 < best_o:
                best_o=o2; best_a=a2
                n=n2
                print
    # perturb loop
    attempt=0
    no_improve_attempts=0
    lowbest = None # maybe keep for blending? We didn't store. Could store if low search done.
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 60:
       attempt +=1
       # remaining time
       rem=deadline - time.monotonic()
       if attempt <=8:
           sub_time=40.0
       elif attempt<=20:
           sub_time=25.0
       elif attempt<=40:
           sub_time=15.0
       else:
           sub_time=10.0
       sub_time=min(sub_time, rem-60)
       if sub_time < 5: break
       # choose start
       if attempt % 7 == 0:
           start_a = best_a.copy(); mode='hard'; eta=0.02; Kmax=4
       else:
           start_a = _perturb_variant(best_a, rng, attempt)
           mode='auto'; eta=0.04; Kmax=8
       # optional if start is much worse, maybe skip? 
       o_start=_obj_n(_project_n(start_a,len(start_a)),len(start_a))
       if o_start > best_o + 0.15 and attempt % 3 !=1:
           # generate a smaller perturbation
           start_a = _project_n(0.7*best_a + 0.3*_perturb_block(best_a,rng,0.1,0.1), n)
           o_start=_obj_n(start_a,n)
       dead = time.monotonic()+sub_time
       new_a,new_o = _run_opt(start_a,n,dead,f'p{attempt}',mode=mode,eta0=eta,Kmax=Kmax,momentum=0.7 if mode=='auto' else 0.3,rng=rng)
       if new_o < best_o - 1e-9:
           best_o=new_o; best_a=new_a.copy(); no_improve_attempts=0
           print
       else:
           no_improve_attempts +=1
           if no_improve_attempts > 15:
              # bigger perturbation by random new init maybe
              start_a = _make_random_init(n, rng)
              # (loop continues)
    # final polish
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 2:
       final_deadline=deadline-1
       best_a,best_o = _run_opt(best_a,n,final_deadline,'final',mode='hard',eta0=0.015,Kmax=2,momentum=0.0,rng=rng)
    # validate with evaluate_sequence
    arr = np.clip(np.asarray(best_a,dtype=float),0,1000)
    arr = np.maximum(arr,0)
    if arr.size==0 or arr.sum()<0.01:
       arr=np.ones(n)
    result=[float(x) for x in arr]
    try:
       val=float(evaluate_sequence(result))
    except Exception:
       val=float('inf')
    if not np.isfinite(val):
       # fallback try uniform
       result=[1.0]*n
       try: val=float(evaluate_sequence(result))
       except: val=2.0
    print final
    return result
```

def _perturb_variant(a,n,rng,attempt):
    b=a.copy()
    kind = int(rng.integers(0,5))
    if kind==0:
        sigma = float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.5 if attempt<20 else 1.0))
        wf = float(rng.uniform(0.01,0.3))
        b=_perturb_block(b,rng,sigma,wf)
    elif kind==1:
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.3,1.5))
        b=_perturb_log(b,rng,8,amp)
    elif kind==2:
        # small roll
        s=int(rng.integers(-max(1,n//20), max(1,n//20)+1))
        b=np.roll(b,s)
    elif kind==3:
        # blend with sym/reverse?
        b=0.5*b + 0.5*a[::-1]
    else:
        # down-up smooth
        m=max(32, n//4)
        b=_resample(_downsample(b,m),n)
        b=_perturb_block(b,rng,0.1,0.2)
    return _project_n(b,n)

def _make_random_init(n,rng):
    kind = int(rng.integers(0,4))
    if kind==0: a=rng.dirichlet(1.0,size=n)
    elif kind==1: x=np.arange(n); a=1+0.5*cos + noise
    elif kind==2: a=np.exp(-np.abs(x-n*0.3)/n) + 1e-3
    else: a=np.exp(rng.normal(0,1,n))+1e-3
    return _project_n(a,n)
```

def _downsample(a,N):
    n=len(a)
    if N==n: return a.copy()
    if N<=0: return np.array([])
    cuts = np.floor(np.arange(N+1)*n/N).astype(np.int64)
    if cuts[-1] != n: cuts[-1]=n
    # ensure cuts increasing; if duplicates, reduceat issue. Use bincount with indices.
    idx = np.repeat(np.arange(N), np.diff(cuts))
    return np.bincount(idx, weights=a, minlength=N)
```

def _project_n(a,n):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size != n:
        if a.size < n:
            # pad? But should match. Use resample? To be safe, if size mismatch, resize by resample.
            # Avoid calling _resample before defined? It is top-level, order not matter at runtime.
            a=_resample(a,n)
        else:
            a=_downsample(a,n)
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones(n,dtype=np.float64)
    return (a/s)*n
```
But `_project_n` calling `_resample`/`_downsample` which call `_project_n` could recursion. In `_resample`, we call `_project_n` at end. If `_project_n` calls `_resample` due to mismatch, `_resample` calls `_project_n` -> recursion. Avoid mismatch inside `_project_n`: assume correct length. If mismatch, just truncate/pad or use simple linear without project. Better:
```
def _project_n(a,n):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size != n:
       # simple: if smaller pad zeros, if larger cut
       b=np.zeros(n)
       m=min(n,a.size)
       b[:m]=a[:m]
       a=b
    ...
```
No recursion. For resample, call `_project_n` after length correct.

`_resample`:
```
def _resample(a,N):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
    if N<=0: return np.zeros(0)
    if a.size==N: return a.copy()
    m=a.size
    x_old=(np.arange(m)+0.5)/m
    x_new=(np.arange(N)+0.5)/N
    if m==1: out=np.full(N,a[0])
    else: out=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a,left=a[0],right=a[-1])
    return _project_n(out,N)
```
`_project_n` no resample call now.

`_downsample`:
```
def _downsample(a,N):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel(); n=a.size
    if N<=0: return zeros
    if N==n: return a.copy()
    if N>n: return _resample(a,N)
    cuts=np.floor(np.arange(N+1)*n/N).astype(np.int64)
    if cuts[-1] != n: cuts[-1]=n
    counts=np.diff(cuts)
    idx=np.repeat(np.arange(N,dtype=np.int64), counts)
    out=np.bincount(idx, weights=a, minlength=N)
    return _project_n(out,N)
```
If counts zero, repeat okay. `np.bincount` weights length matches. Good.

`_obj_n`:
```
def _obj_n(a,n):
    c=np.convolve(a,a)
    if c.size==0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(c)): return np.inf
    mc=float(np.max(c))
    s=float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 1e-12: return np.inf
    if abs(s - n) <= 1e-6*max(1.0,n):
        return 2.0*mc/n
    return 2.0*n*mc/(s*s)
```
If a sum n, fast branch. `_obj_general` can call `_obj_n` with n=a.size.

`_obj_general(a)`:
```
def _obj_general(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
    if a.size==0: return np.inf
    return _obj_n(a,a.size)
```

`_softmax_weights`:
```
def _softmax_weights(c,tau):
    c=np.asarray(c,float)
    M=float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(M): return np.zeros_like(c)
    m=len(c)
    if tau <= 1e-12:
       p=np.zeros(m)
       idx=np.where(c >= M - 1e-9*max(1.0,M))[0]
       if idx.size==0: idx=np.array([int(np.argmax(c))])
       p[idx]=1.0/idx.size
       return p
    e=np.exp((c-M)/tau)
    s=float(e.sum())
    if s < 1e-300:
       p=zeros top
    else:
       p=e/s
    return p
```
Use `np.isfinite(M)`? M finite.

`_select_indices`:
```
def _select_indices(c,n,Kmax):
    m=len(c); L=m-1; M=float(c.max()); tol=max(1e-9*max(1.0,n), 0.02*M)
    order=np.argsort(c)[::-1].astype(int)
    seen=np.zeros(m,dtype=bool)
    out=[]
    first=True
    for k in order:
       pk=L-int(k)
       if seen[k] or seen[pk]: continue
       if not first:
          if c[k] < M - tol:
             break
       # For first, add top pair
       out.append(int(k)); out.append(pk)
       seen[int(k)]=True; seen[pk]=True
       first=False
       if len(out) >= Kmax:
          break
    if len(out)==0:
       k=int(np.argmax(c)); out=[k,L-k]
    # If Kmax > len and we broke due to tol but want more, maybe fill with next top pairs up to Kmax? Could be beneficial. Let's fill without tol if len < Kmax:
    if len(out) < Kmax:
       for k in order:
          pk=L-int(k)
          if seen[k] or seen[pk]: continue
          out.append(int(k)); out.append(pk); seen[int(k)]=True; seen[pk]=True
          if len(out) >= Kmax: break
    return np.array(out[:Kmax], dtype=int)
```
But after fill without tol, might include far peaks and dilute. Maybe okay if Kmax requested. For top1 Kmax=2 no fill. For Kmax=8, if only top pair nearby, might fill with next best. Fine.

`_grad_hard_multi`:
```
def _grad_hard_multi(a,idx,n):
    g=np.zeros(n,dtype=np.float64)
    for k in idx:
       k=int(k)
       js=max(0,k-n+1)
       je=min(n-1,k)+1
       if js < je:
          ii=k - np.arange(js,je,dtype=np.int64)
          g[js:je] += 2.0*a[ii]
    return g
```
Need unique idx? `_select_indices` can have duplicate if k=pk (center) and we add twice? For center k=L/2, pk=k. In select, seen prevents adding twice? We add k and pk, set both. If k=pk, out has duplicate. Then gradient adds twice. Since if a single center peak, its pair is itself; subgradient over one coefficient not two duplicates. We should avoid duplicates. In select:
```
if pk == int(k): out.append(int(k)); seen[k]=True
else: out.append(k); out.append(pk); seen...
```
Do that. For symmetric peaks, center one. Then len may be odd. Good.

`_grad_smooth`: if p length !=2n-1, maybe resize? But okay.

`_stepped_objective` top-level:
```
def _stepped_objective(a,d,step,n):
    cand = _project_n(a - step*d, n)
    return cand, _obj_n(cand,n)
```

`_run_opt` exact:
```
def _run_opt(a,n,deadline,tag,rng,mode='auto',eta0=0.04,...):
    a=_project_n(a,n); n=int(n)
    best=a.copy(); best_o=_obj_n(a,n)
    v=np.zeros(n)
    eta=eta0
    last_improve=time.monotonic()
    last_print=last_improve
    t_start=last_improve
    duration=max(1.0, deadline - t_start)
    it=0
    if not np.isfinite(best_o):
       best_o=2.0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       now=time.monotonic()
       if now >= deadline: break
       c=np.convolve(a,a)
       if c.size==0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(c)): break
       mc=float(np.max(c)); o=2.0*mc/n
       if not np.isfinite(o): break
       improved_best = (o < best_o - 1e-12)
       if improved_best:
          best_o=o; best=a.copy(); last_improve=now
       # fringe print
       if improved_best and (now - last_print > 5.0):
          print(f"[{tag}] t={now-t_start:.0f}s obj={best_o:.6f} eta={eta:.4f}")
          last_print=now
       # stuck jitter
       if now - last_improve > max(20.0, duration*0.15) and now - t_start > max(10.0, duration*0.1):
           if o > best_o*1.02:
               a=best.copy()
           else:
               a=_perturb_block(a,rng,0.05,0.04)
           v.fill(0.0); eta=min(eta,0.03); last_improve=now; continue
       frac=min(1.0, (now-t_start)/duration)
       use_smooth = (mode=='smooth') or (mode=='auto' and frac < 0.25)
       if use_smooth:
           if mode=='auto':
              local_frac=min(1.0, frac/0.25)
           else:
              local_frac=frac
           tau_frac=tau0_frac + (tau_end_frac-tau0_frac)*local_frac
           tau=max(1e-6, n*tau_frac)
           p=_softmax_weights(c,tau)
           g=_grad_smooth(p,a)
       else:
           Kcur=Kmax
           if Kmax > 2:
              if frac < 0.35: Kcur=2
              elif frac < 0.65: Kcur=min(Kmax, max(2, int(round(Kmax*0.5))))
           idx=_select_indices(c,n,Kcur)
           g=_grad_hard_multi(a,idx,n)
       g-=g.mean()
       gm=float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
       if gm < 1e-13:
           break
       d=g/gm
       if momentum>0:
           v=momentum*v + (1.0-momentum)*d
           d=v.copy()
           d-=d.mean()
           gmv=float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
           if gmv < 1e-13: break
           d=d/gmv
       cand, oc = _stepped_objective(a,d,eta,n)
       if oc < o - 1e-12:
           a=cand
           if oc < best_o - 1e-12:
              best_o=oc; best=a.copy(); last_improve=now
           eta=min(eta*1.03, eta_max)
       else:
           if line_search:
              cand2, oc2 = _stepped_objective(a,d,0.5*eta,n)
              if oc2 < o - 1e-12:
                  a=cand2
                  if oc2 < best_o - 1e-12: best_o=oc2; best=a.copy(); last_improve=now
                  eta=min(eta, eta_max)
              else:
                  eta=max(eta*0.85, eta_min)
           else:
              eta=max(eta*0.85, eta_min)
       it +=1
       if max_iters is not None and it >= max_iters: break
    return best,best_o
```
Potential: If objective improves but not best? impossible if o<best? We update best at start of loop based on current o; but after step if oc < best_o, update. Good.

If initial o maybe >best? best initialized.

No lambdas. f-string okay.

`_perturb_block`:
```
def _perturb_block(a,rng,sigma,width_frac):
    n=len(a); b=a.copy()
    w=max(2, int(n*width_frac))
    if w >= n: w=n
    s=int(rng.integers(0, n-w+1))
    b[s:s+w] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=w))
    return _project_n(b,n)
```
If sigma huge, exp overflow? sigma up to 1, size w, exp ~e^3 if 3sd; okay. For attempt bigger sigma 1.5, exp maybe 100; okay. Could clip noise to [-5,5].

`_perturb_log`:
```
def _perturb_log(a,n,rng,max_mode,amp):
    w=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-12))
    i=np.arange(n)
    for _ in range(2):
       m=int(rng.integers(1,max_mode+1))
       ph=float(rng.uniform(0.0,2.0*np.pi))
       co=float(rng.normal(0.0,amp))
       w += co*np.sin(2.0*np.pi*m*i/n + ph)
    out=np.exp(w - np.max(w))
    return _project_n(out,n)
```
No nested loops? for okay.

`_make_variants`:
```
def _make_variants(a,n,rng):
    res=[a.copy(), _project_n((a+a[::-1])*0.5,n)]
    shifts=[n//8, -n//8, n//3, -n//3, n//16]
    for s in shifts:
       res.append(_project_n(np.roll(a,s),n))
    # taper? no
    return res
```
Need `np.roll` with negative shift works.

`_make_random_init`:
```
def _make_random_init(n,rng):
    kind=int(rng.integers(0,5))
    x=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
    if kind==0:
       a=rng.dirichlet(1.0,size=n)
    elif kind==1:
       a=1.0 + 0.5*np.sin(2*np.pi*3*x/n + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
    elif kind==2:
       center=float(rng.uniform(0.2,0.8))*n
       a=np.exp(-np.abs(x-center)/(n*0.2))
    elif kind==3:
       a=np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,1.0,size=n))
    else:
       a=np.ones(n); a[::2]*=1.5; a[1::2]*=0.5
    return _project_n(a,n)
```
Dirichlet size n with alpha 1 samples uniform simplex sum1; project to n. For n=3809, okay.

`_low_res_search`:
```
def _low_res_search(target_n, seed_a, rng, deadline, budget_s, use_lp=True):
    if linprog is None: use_lp=False
    if use_lp:
       m=max(32, min(128, target_n))
    else:
       m=max(128, min(256, target_n))
    if m > target_n: m=target_n
    inits=[]
    if seed_a is not None and len(seed_a)>0:
       inits.append(_downsample(seed_a,m))
    x=np.arange(m,dtype=float)
    inits.append(np.ones(m,dtype=float))
    inits.append(_project_n(x+1.0,m))
    inits.append(_project_n(m-x+1.0,m))
    inits.append(_project_n(rng.dirichlet(0.5,size=m),m))
    p=np.ones(m); p[1::2]=0.4; inits.append(_project_n(p,m))
    p=1.0+0.5*np.sin(2*np.pi*3*x/m); inits.append(_project_n(p,m))
    p=1.0+0.6*np.cos(2*np.pi*2*x/m); inits.append(_project_n(p,m))
    inits.append(_make_random_init(m,rng))
    per=max(5.0, (budget_s - 5.0)/max(1,len(inits)))
    best=None; best_o=np.inf
    for idx in range(len(inits)):
       sub=min(time.monotonic()+per, deadline-5.0)
       if sub <= time.monotonic(): continue
       a1,o1=_run_opt(inits[idx], m, sub, 'low'+str(idx), rng, mode='auto', eta0=0.05, tau0_frac=0.2, tau_end_frac=0.02, Kmax=8, momentum=0.7, line_search=True)
       if o1 < best_o:
          best_o=o1; best=a1.copy()
    if best is None or not np.isfinite(best_o):
       return None
    if use_lp and linprog is not None:
       lp_deadline = min(deadline - 5.0, time.monotonic() + max(5.0, min(20.0, (budget_s*0.25))))
       if lp_deadline > time.monotonic() + 2:
          best,_ = _lp_refine(best, m, lp_deadline, max_iters=30)
    return best
```

```
def _lp_refine(a,n,deadline,max_iters=30):
    a=_project_n(a,n); best=a.copy(); best_o=_obj_n(a,n)
    it=0
    while time.monotonic()<deadline and it<max_iters:
       a=np.maximum(a,1e-6)
       s=float(a.sum()); if s<=0: break
       a=a/s*n
       c=np.convolve(a,a); M=float(c.max()); o=2*M/n
       if o<best_o -1e-10: best_o=o; best=a.copy()
       # build C
       C=np.zeros((2*n-1,n),dtype=np.float64)
       for k in range(2*n-1):
          lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
          if lo<=hi:
             C[k,lo:hi+1] = a[k - np.arange(lo,hi+1,dtype=np.int64)]
       minf=float(np.min(a))
       ub=max(1e6, M/(minf+1e-12))
       obj=-np.ones(n)
       bvec=M*np.ones(2*n-1)
       try:
          res=linprog(obj, A_ub=C, b_ub=bvec, bounds=(0.0, ub), method='highs', options={'disp': False})
       except Exception:
          res=None
       if res is None or getattr(res,'x',None) is None:
          break
       g=np.asarray(res.x,dtype=float)
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)) or g.size!=n:
          break
       g=np.maximum(g,0)
       gs=float(g.sum())
       if gs <= 1e-12:
          break
       g=g/gs*n
       # line search t
       best_cand=a.copy(); best_cand_o=o
       for t in (0.3,0.5,0.8,1.0):
          cand=_project_n((1.0-t)*a + t*g, n)
          co=_obj_n(cand,n)
          if co < best_cand_o - 1e-12:
             best_cand_o=co; best_cand=cand
       if best_cand_o >= o - 1e-10:
          # no improvement, maybe try next? break if several no improve
          # We'll break after 3 failed?
          break
       a=best_cand
       if best_cand_o < best_o - 1e-10:
          best_o=best_cand_o; best=a.copy()
          print(f"[lp] obj={best_o:.6f}")
       it +=1
       if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 2: break
    return best,best_o
```

```
"""..."""
import numpy as np
import time
try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception:
    linprog = None

def _project_n(a,n):
    a=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size != n:
        b=np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        m=min(n, a.size)
        if m>0:
            b[:m]=a[:m]
        a=b
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return (a/s)*float(n)
...
```

`_select_indices`: Need `c` is np array; `len(c)` maybe 2n-1. If n=1, L=0, center. In fill loop, if pk same. Good.
```
def _select_indices(c,n,Kmax):
    m=len(c)
    if m==0: return np.array([],dtype=int)
    L=m-1
    M=float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(M):
        return np.array([int(np.argmax(c))],dtype=int)
    tol=max(1e-9*max(1.0,float(n)), 0.02*abs(M))
    order=np.argsort(c)[::-1].astype(np.int64)
    seen=np.zeros(m, dtype=bool)
    out=[]
    first=True
    for kval in order:
        k=int(kval)
        pk=L-k
        if seen[k] or seen[pk]:
            continue
        if not first and c[k] < M - tol:
            break
        if pk == k:
            out.append(k)
            seen[k]=True
        else:
            out.append(k); out.append(pk)
            seen[k]=True; seen[pk]=True
        first=False
        if len(out) >= Kmax:
            break
    if len(out) == 0:
        k=int(np.argmax(c))
        out.append(k)
        pk=L-k
        if pk != k: out.append(pk)
    if len(out) < Kmax:
        for kval in order:
            k=int(kval); pk=L-k
            if seen[k] or seen[pk]: continue
            if pk == k:
                out.append(k); seen[k]=True
            else:
                out.append(k); out.append(pk); seen[k]=True; seen[pk]=True
            if len(out) >= Kmax: break
    if len(out) > Kmax:
        out=out[:Kmax]
    return np.array(out, dtype=np.int64)
```
The second fill may add far peaks; okay.

`_grad_hard_multi`: if idx empty return zeros.

```
def _lp_refine(...):
    ...
    try:
       res=linprog(obj, A_ub=C, b_ub=bvec, bounds=(0.0,ub), method='highs', options={'disp':False})
    except Exception:
       try:
          res=linprog(obj, A_ub=C, b_ub=bvec, bounds=(0.0,ub), options={'disp':False})
       except Exception:
          res=None
```

```python
"""
Search for a non-negative polynomial coefficient sequence minimizing
    2 * n * max(coefficients of (A(z)^2)) / (sum(coefficients))**2.

The method uses:
  - projected multiplicative/subgradient optimization on the simplex,
  - active-set top-peak hard subgradients,
  - soft log-sum-exp weighting over upper-tail convolution peaks,
  - low-resolution multi-start preoptimization,
  - optional low-resolution LP Frank-Wolfe-type refinement (HiGHS/linprog),
  - random restarts / perturbations and final hard active-set polish.

All helpers are top-level and use no closures or lambdas.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception:
    linprog = None


def _project_n(a, n):
    """Project a 1D array onto {x >= 0, sum x = n}."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size != n:
        b = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        m = min(n, a.size)
        if m > 0:
            b[:m] = a[:m]
        a = b
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return (a / s) * float(n)


def _resample(a, N):
    """Resample a nonnegative sequence to length N by linear interpolation."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if N <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    m = a.size
    if m == 0:
        return np.ones(N, dtype=np.float64)
    if m == N:
        return np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    x_old = (np.arange(m, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(m)
    x_new = (np.arange(N, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(N)
    if m == 1:
        out = np.full(N, float(a[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a, left=a[0], right=a[-1])
    return _project_n(out, N)


def _downsample(a, N):
    """Downsample a nonnegative sequence to length N by block sums."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if N <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.ones(N, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == N:
        return np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    if n < N:
        return _resample(a, N)
    cuts = np.floor(np.arange(N + 1, dtype=np.int64) * n / N).astype(np.int64)
    cuts[-1] = n
    counts = np.diff(cuts)
    idx = np.repeat(np.arange(N, dtype=np.int64), counts)
    out = np.bincount(idx, weights=np.maximum(a, 0.0), minlength=N)
    return _project_n(out, N)


def _obj_n(a, n):
    """Objective for a sequence assumed to have sum n; also robust if not."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return float('inf')
    c = np.convolve(a, a)
    if c.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
        return float('inf')
    mc = float(np.max(c))
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return float('inf')
    if abs(s - n) <= 1e-6 * max(1.0, float(n)):
        return 2.0 * mc / float(n)
    return 2.0 * float(n) * mc / (s * s)


def _obj_general(a):
    """Objective for arbitrary length nonnegative sequence."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return float('inf')
    return _obj_n(a, a.size)


def _softmax_weights(c, tau):
    """Stable softmax weights over convolution coefficients."""
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    if c.size == 0:
        return c.copy()
    M = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(M):
        return np.zeros_like(c, dtype=np.float64)
    m = c.size
    if tau <= 1e-12:
        p = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
        tol = 1e-9 * max(1.0, abs(M))
        idx = np.where(c >= M - tol)[0]
        if idx.size == 0:
            idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(c))], dtype=np.int64)
        p[idx] = 1.0 / float(idx.size)
        return p
    e = np.exp((c - M) / tau)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s < 1e-300:
        p = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
        idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(c))], dtype=np.int64)
        p[idx] = 1.0
        return p
    return e / s


def _select_indices(c, n, Kmax):
    """Select up to Kmax significant symmetric peak indices in c."""
    m = len(c)
    if m == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.int64)
    L = m - 1
    M = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(M):
        return np.array([int(np.argmax(c))], dtype=np.int64)
    tol = max(1e-9 * max(1.0, float(n)), 0.02 * abs(M))
    order = np.argsort(c)[::-1].astype(np.int64)
    seen = np.zeros(m, dtype=bool)
    out = []
    first = True
    for kval in order:
        k = int(kval)
        pk = L - k
        if seen[k] or seen[pk]:
            continue
        if not first and c[k] < M - tol:
            break
        if pk == k:
            out.append(k)
            seen[k] = True
        else:
            out.append(k)
            out.append(pk)
            seen[k] = True
            seen[pk] = True
        first = False
        if len(out) >= Kmax:
            break

    if len(out) == 0:
        k = int(np.argmax(c))
        out.append(k)
        pk = L - k
        if pk != k:
            out.append(pk)

    if len(out) < Kmax:
        for kval in order:
            k = int(kval)
            pk = L - k
            if seen[k] or seen[pk]:
                continue
            if pk == k:
                out.append(k)
                seen[k] = True
            else:
                out.append(k)
                out.append(pk)
                seen[k] = True
                seen[pk] = True
            if len(out) >= Kmax:
                break

    if len(out) > Kmax:
        out = out[:Kmax]
    return np.array(out, dtype=np.int64)


def _grad_hard_multi(a, idx, n):
    """Subgradient contribution from selected active convolution peaks."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for kval in idx:
        k = int(kval)
        js = max(0, k - n + 1)
        je = min(n - 1, k) + 1
        if js < je:
            ii = k - np.arange(js, je, dtype=np.int64)
            g[js:je] += 2.0 * a[ii]
    return g


def _grad_smooth(p, a):
    """Gradient of weighted sum of coefficients wrt sequence."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    expected = 2 * a.size - 1
    if p.size != expected:
        return np.zeros(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return 2.0 * np.correlate(p, a, mode='valid')


def _stepped_objective(a, d, step, n):
    """Evaluate a project-and-step candidate."""
    cand = _project_n(a - step * d, n)
    return cand, _obj_n(cand, n)


def _run_opt(a, n, deadline, tag, rng,
             mode='auto',
             eta0=0.04,
             eta_min=1e-4,
             eta_max=0.20,
             tau0_frac=0.15,
             tau_end_frac=0.01,
             Kmax=8,
             momentum=0.7,
             line_search=False,
             eval_step=False,
             max_iters=None):
    """Time-limited local optimizer. Returns (best_sequence, best_objective)."""
    n = int(n)
    a = _project_n(a, n)
    best = a.copy()
    best_o = _obj_n(a, n)
    if not np.isfinite(best_o):
        best = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        best_o = _obj_n(best, n)

    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    eta = float(eta0)
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = float(deadline)
    if deadline <= t_start + 1e-3:
        return best, best_o

    duration = max(1.0, deadline - t_start)
    last_improve = t_start
    last_print = -1e9
    prev_o = None
    bad_count = 0
    it = 0
    max_iter_allowed = int(max_iters) if max_iters is not None else -1

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= deadline:
            break

        c = np.convolve(a, a)
        if c.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
            break
        mc = float(np.max(c))
        o = 2.0 * mc / float(n)
        if not np.isfinite(o):
            break

        if o < best_o - 1e-12:
            best_o = o
            best = a.copy()
            last_improve = now
            bad_count = 0
            if now - last_print > 8.0:
                print(f"[{tag}] t={now - t_start:.0f}s obj={best_o:.6f} eta={eta:.4f}")
                last_print = now

        if prev_o is not None and not eval_step:
            if o < prev_o - 1e-12:
                eta = min(eta * 1.03, eta_max)
                bad_count = 0
            elif o > prev_o + 1e-12:
                eta = max(eta * 0.90, eta_min)
                bad_count += 1
            else:
                bad_count = 0

        if (not eval_step) and bad_count >= 3 and best_o < np.inf and o > best_o * 1.02:
            a = best.copy()
            v.fill(0.0)
            eta = min(eta, 0.02)
            prev_o = best_o
            bad_count = 0
            continue

        if (now - last_improve) > max(20.0, 0.15 * duration) and (now - t_start) > max(10.0, 0.10 * duration):
            if o > best_o * 1.02:
                a = best.copy()
            else:
                a = _perturb_block(a, rng, 0.05, 0.04)
            v.fill(0.0)
            eta = min(eta, 0.03)
            prev_o = None
            bad_count = 0
            last_improve = now
            continue

        frac = min(1.0, (now - t_start) / duration)
        use_smooth = (mode == 'smooth') or (mode == 'auto' and frac < 0.25)

        if use_smooth:
            if mode == 'auto':
                local_frac = min(1.0, frac / 0.25)
            else:
                local_frac = frac
            tau_frac = tau0_frac + (tau_end_frac - tau0_frac) * local_frac
            tau = max(1e-6, float(n) * tau_frac)
            p = _softmax_weights(c, tau)
            g = _grad_smooth(p, a)
        else:
            Kcur = int(Kmax)
            if Kcur > 2:
                if frac < 0.35:
                    Kcur = 2
                elif frac < 0.65:
                    Kcur = min(Kcur, max(2, int(round(0.5 * Kcur))))
            idx = _select_indices(c, n, Kcur)
            g = _grad_hard_multi(a, idx, n)

        g -= g.mean()
        gm = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
        if gm < 1e-13:
            break
        d = g / gm

        if momentum > 0.0:
            v = momentum * v + (1.0 - momentum) * d
            d = v.copy()
            d -= d.mean()
            gmv = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
            if gmv < 1e-13:
                break
            d = d / gmv

        if eval_step:
            cand, oc = _stepped_objective(a, d, eta, n)
            if oc < o - 1e-12:
                a = cand
                if oc < best_o - 1e-12:
                    best_o = oc
                    best = a.copy()
                    last_improve = time.monotonic()
                eta = min(eta * 1.03, eta_max)
                prev_o = o
            else:
                accepted = False
                if line_search:
                    cand2, oc2 = _stepped_objective(a, d, 0.5 * eta, n)
                    if oc2 < o - 1e-12:
                        a = cand2
                        if oc2 < best_o - 1e-12:
                            best_o = oc2
                            best = a.copy()
                            last_improve = time.monotonic()
                        accepted = True
                        eta = min(eta, eta_max)
                if not accepted:
                    eta = max(eta * 0.85, eta_min)
                prev_o = o
        else:
            a = _project_n(a - eta * d, n)
            prev_o = o

        it += 1
        if max_iter_allowed > 0 and it >= max_iter_allowed:
            break

    c = np.convolve(best, best)
    if c.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
        o = 2.0 * float(np.max(c)) / float(n)
        if np.isfinite(o) and o < best_o:
            best_o = o
    return best, best_o


def _perturb_block(a, rng, sigma, width_frac):
    """Multiplicative random block perturbation."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    b = a.copy()
    w = max(2, int(n * float(width_frac)))
    w = min(w, n)
    s = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - w + 1)))
    noise = rng.normal(0.0, float(sigma), size=w)
    noise = np.clip(noise, -5.0, 5.0)
    b[s:s + w] *= np.exp(noise)
    return _project_n(b, n)


def _perturb_log(a, rng, max_mode, amp):
    """Perturb in log-domain with random low-frequency sinusoids."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    w = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-12))
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for _ in range(2):
        m = int(rng.integers(1, max_mode + 1))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        co = float(rng.normal(0.0, float(amp)))
        w += co * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * float(m) * i / float(n) + ph)
    out = np.exp(w - float(np.max(w)))
    return _project_n(out, n)


def _blend_sequences(a, b, w):
    """Convex blend of two same-length sequences."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if b.size != n:
        b = _resample(b, n)
    return _project_n((1.0 - w) * a + w * b, n)


def _perturb_variant(a, rng, attempt):
    """Create a different candidate from the current best."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    kind = int(rng.integers(0, 5))
    if kind == 0:
        base_sigma = 0.45 if attempt < 20 else 1.00
        sigma = float(rng.uniform(0.05, base_sigma))
        wf = float(rng.uniform(0.01, 0.30))
        b = _perturb_block(a, rng, sigma, wf)
    elif kind == 1:
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.3, 1.5))
        b = _perturb_log(a, rng, 8, amp)
    elif kind == 2:
        sh = int(rng.integers(-max(1, n // 20), max(1, n // 20) + 1))
        b = np.roll(a, sh)
    elif kind == 3:
        b = 0.5 * a + 0.5 * a[::-1]
    else:
        m = max(32, n // 4)
        b = _resample(_downsample(a, m), n)
        b = _perturb_block(b, rng, 0.10, 0.20)
    return _project_n(b, n)


def _make_variants(a, n, rng):
    """Quick deterministic variants for the initial startup."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    res = [a.copy()]
    res.append(_project_n(0.5 * (a + a[::-1]), n))
    shifts = [n // 8, -n // 8, n // 3, -n // 3]
    for s in shifts:
        res.append(_project_n(np.roll(a, int(s)), n))
    return res


def _make_random_init(n, rng):
    """Random feasible initializer."""
    kind = int(rng.integers(0, 5))
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if kind == 0:
        a = rng.dirichlet(1.0, size=n)
    elif kind == 1:
        a = 1.0 + 0.5 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * 3.0 * x / n + float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)))
    elif kind == 2:
        center = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 0.8)) * n
        a = np.exp(-np.abs(x - center) / (0.2 * n + 1.0))
    elif kind == 3:
        a = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=n))
    else:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        a[::2] *= 1.5
        a[1::2] *= 0.5
    return _project_n(a, n)


def _lp_refine(a, n, deadline, max_iters=30):
    """LP Frank-Wolfe-type refine on a small sequence. Conservative: skips if too slow/fails."""
    a = _project_n(a, int(n), n)
    best = a.copy()
    best_o = _obj_n(a, n)
    if linprog is None:
        return best, best_o

    it = 0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline and it < max_iters:
        a = np.maximum(a, 1e-6)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s <= 1e-15:
            break
        a = (a / s) * float(n)
        c = np.convolve(a, a)
        if c.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
            break
        M = float(np.max(c))
        o = 2.0 * M / float(n)
        if not np.isfinite(o):
            break
        if o < best_o - 1e-12:
            best_o = o
            best = a.copy()

        C = np.zeros((2 * n - 1, n), dtype=np.float64)
        for k in range(2 * n - 1):
            lo = max(0, k - n + 1)
            hi = min(n - 1, k)
            if lo <= hi:
                C[k, lo:hi + 1] = a[k - np.arange(lo, hi + 1, dtype=np.int64)]

        minf = float(np.min(a))
        ub = max(1e6, M / (minf + 1e-12))
        objvec = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        bvec = M * np.ones(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64)

        res = None
        try:
            res = linprog(objvec, A_ub=C, b_ub=bvec, bounds=(0.0, ub),
                          method='highs', options={'disp': False})
        except Exception:
            try:
                res = linprog(objvec, A_ub=C, b_ub=bvec, bounds=(0.0, ub),
                              options={'disp': False})
            except Exception:
                res = None

        if res is None or getattr(res, 'x', None) is None:
            break
        g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        if g.size != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
            break
        g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
        gs = float(g.sum())
        if gs <= 1e-12:
            break
        g = (g / gs) * float(n)

        cand_best = a.copy()
        cand_best_o = o
        for t in (0.30, 0.50, 0.80, 1.00):
            cand = _project_n((1.0 - t) * a + t * g, n)
            co = _obj_n(cand, n)
            if np.isfinite(co) and co < cand_best_o - 1e-12:
                cand_best_o = co
                cand_best = cand

        if cand_best_o >= o - 1e-10:
            break

        a = cand_best
        if best_o > cand_best_o:
            best_o = cand_best_o
            best = a.copy()
            print(f"[lp] n={n} obj={best_o:.6f} it={it}")
        it += 1
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 2.0:
            break

    return best, best_o


def _low_res_search(target_n, seed_a, rng, deadline, budget_s, use_lp):
    """Small-scale multi-start optimizer to create a good global-ish initializer."""
    if linprog is None:
        use_lp = False
    if use_lp:
        m = max(32, min(128, int(target_n)))
    else:
        m = max(128, min(256, int(target_n)))
    m = max(1, int(m))

    inits = []
    if seed_a is not None and len(seed_a) > 0:
        inits.append(_downsample(seed_a, m))

    x = np.arange(m, dtype=np.float64)
    inits.append(np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64))
    inits.append(_project_n(x + 1.0, m))
    inits.append(_project_n(m - x + 1.0, m))
    inits.append(_project_n(rng.dirichlet(0.5, size=m), m))

    p = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64)
    p[1::2] = 0.40
    inits.append(_project_n(p, m))

    p = 1.0 + 0.5 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * 3.0 * x / m)
    inits.append(_project_n(p, m))

    p = 1.0 + 0.6 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * 2.0 * x / m)
    inits.append(_project_n(p, m))

    inits.append(_make_random_init(m, rng))

    per = max(5.0, max(1.0, budget_s - 5.0) / max(1, len(inits)))
    best = None
    best_o = float('inf')

    for j in range(len(inits)):
        sub_deadline = min(time.monotonic() + per, deadline - 5.0)
        if sub_deadline <= time.monotonic():
            continue
        a1, o1 = _run_opt(inits[j], m, sub_deadline, f"low{j}", rng,
                          mode='auto',
                          eta0=0.05,
                          tau0_frac=0.20,
                          tau_end_frac=0.02,
                          Kmax=8,
                          momentum=0.70,
                          line_search=True,
                          eval_step=True)
        if np.isfinite(o1) and o1 < best_o:
            best_o = o1
            best = a1.copy()

    if best is None:
        return None

    if use_lp and linprog is not None:
        lp_deadline = min(deadline - 5.0,
                          time.monotonic() + max(5.0, min(25.0, 0.25 * max(1.0, budget_s))))
        if lp_deadline > time.monotonic() + 2.0:
            best, _ = _lp_refine(best, m, lp_deadline, max_iters=30)

    return best


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Main search entry point.
    Returns a Python list of nonnegative floats minimizing evaluate_sequence.
    """
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    budget_s = float(budget_s)
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max(120.0, budget_s) - 5.0

    parent = None
    n = 3809
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if p.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
            p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if float(p.sum()) > 1e-6:
                parent = p
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    init = None
    if parent is None:
        init = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        if parent.size < 64 or parent.size > 8192:
            init = _resample(parent, 3809)
            n = 3809
        else:
            n = int(parent.size)
            init = _project_n(parent, n)

    a = _project_n(init, n)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_o = _obj_n(a, n)
    if not np.isfinite(best_o):
        best_a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        best_o = 2.0
    print(f"initial n={n} obj={best_o:.6f}")

    # Quick deterministic variants.
    for v in _make_variants(best_a, n, rng):
        v = _project_n(v, n)
        ov = _obj_n(v, n)
        if np.isfinite(ov) and ov < best_o:
            best_o = ov
            best_a = v.copy()
            print(f"quick variant n={n} obj={best_o:.6f}")

    # Main first polish, using hard active-set on the current best.
    main_deadline = min(start + 180.0, deadline - 120.0)
    if main_deadline > time.monotonic() + 10.0:
        best_a, best_o = _run_opt(
            best_a, n, main_deadline, "main_hard", rng,
            mode='hard', eta0=0.025, eta_max=0.12, Kmax=6,
            momentum=0.30, eval_step=False)
        print(f"after main_hard n={n} obj={best_o:.6f}")

    # Low-resolution exploration, especially if not already close to target.
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if np.isfinite(best_o) and best_o > 1.515 and remaining > 750.0:
        low_budget = min(120.0, max(60.0, remaining - 900.0))
        if low_budget > 30.0:
            low_dead = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + low_budget)
            low_a = _low_res_search(n, best_a, rng, low_dead, low_budget,
                                    use_lp=(linprog is not None))
            if low_a is not None:
                hi = _resample(low_a, n)
                oh = _obj_n(hi, n)
                if np.isfinite(oh) and oh < best_o + 0.08:
                    sub_dead = min(time.monotonic() + 140.0, deadline - 180.0)
                    if sub_dead > time.monotonic() + 10.0:
                        hi, oh = _run_opt(
                            hi, n, sub_dead, "fromlow", rng,
                            mode='auto', eta0=0.04, Kmax=8,
                            momentum=0.70, eval_step=False)
                        if np.isfinite(oh) and oh < best_o:
                            best_o = oh
                            best_a = hi.copy()
                            n = len(best_a)
                            print(f"from low_res n={n} obj={best_o:.6f}")

    # Try a slightly larger length if beneficial and time permits.
    n = len(best_a)
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if np.isfinite(best_o) and best_o > 1.503 and n < 4096 and remaining > 420.0:
        n2 = 1
        while n2 < n:
            n2 *= 2
        if n2 == n:
            n2 = min(8192, n * 2)
        if n2 <= 8192 and n2 > n:
            a2 = _resample(best_a, n2)
            o2 = _obj_n(a2, n2)
            if np.isfinite(o2) and o2 < best_o + 0.12:
                sub_dead = min(time.monotonic() + 180.0, deadline - 220.0)
                if sub_dead > time.monotonic() + 15.0:
                    a2, o2 = _run_opt(
                        a2, n2, sub_dead, "bigger", rng,
                        mode='auto', eta0=0.04, Kmax=8,
                        momentum=0.70, eval_step=False)
                    if np.isfinite(o2) and o2 < best_o:
                        best_o = o2
                        best_a = a2
                        n = n2
                        print(f"larger length n={n2} obj={best_o:.6f}")

    # Random restart / perturbation loop.
    attempt = 0
    no_improve = 0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 70.0:
        attempt += 1
        n = len(best_a)
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()

        if attempt <= 6:
            sub_time = 45.0
        elif attempt <= 16:
            sub_time = 28.0
        elif attempt <= 32:
            sub_time = 18.0
        else:
            sub_time = 12.0
        sub_time = min(sub_time, max(6.0, rem - 70.0))
        if sub_time <= 5.0:
            break

        if attempt % 7 == 1:
            start_a = best_a.copy()
            mode = 'hard'
            eta = 0.02
            Kmax = 4
            mom = 0.30
        else:
            start_a = _perturb_variant(best_a, rng, attempt)
            os = _obj_n(start_a, n)
            if not np.isfinite(os) or os > best_o + 0.20:
                start_a = _blend_sequences(best_a, _perturb_block(best_a, rng, 0.08, 0.08), 0.30)
                os = _obj_n(start_a, n)
            if not np.isfinite(os) or os > best_o + 0.35:
                continue
            mode = 'auto'
            eta = 0.04
            Kmax = 8
            mom = 0.70

        sub_dead = time.monotonic() + sub_time
        new_a, new_o = _run_opt(
            start_a, n, sub_dead, f"p{attempt}", rng,
            mode=mode, eta0=eta, Kmax=Kmax, momentum=mom,
            eval_step=False)

        if np.isfinite(new_o) and new_o < best_o - 1e-10:
            best_o = new_o
            best_a = new_a
            no_improve = 0
            print(f"perturb {attempt}: new obj={best_o:.6f} n={len(best_a)}")
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve >= 18:
                start_a = _make_random_init(n, rng)
                new_a, new_o = _run_opt(
                    start_a, n, time.monotonic() + min(20.0, rem - 80.0),
                    f"rand{attempt}", rng,
                    mode='auto', eta0=0.06, Kmax=8, momentum=0.8,
                    line_search=False, eval_step=False)
                if np.isfinite(new_o) and new_o < best_o:
                    best_o = new_o
                    best_a = new_a
                    no_improve = 0
                    print(f"random restart {attempt}: obj={best_o:.6f}")
                else:
                    no_improve = 0

    # Final polish.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 2.0:
        n = len(best_a)
        best_a, best_o = _run_opt(
            best_a, n, deadline - 1.0, "final", rng,
            mode='hard', eta0=0.015, eta_max=0.08, Kmax=2,
            momentum=0.0, eval_step=False)
        print(f"final polished obj={best_o:.6f} n={n}")

    # Build a valid list.
    arr = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64)
    if arr.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)) or float(np.sum(np.maximum(arr, 0.0))) < 0.01:
        arr = np.ones(max(1, int(n)), dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)

    result = [float(x) for x in arr]
    try:
        val = float(evaluate_sequence(result))
    except Exception:
        val = float('inf')

    if not np.isfinite(val):
        fallback = [1.0] * max(1, int(len(result)))
        try:
            val2 = float(evaluate_sequence(fallback))
            if np.isfinite(val2):
                result = fallback
        except Exception:
            pass

    print(f"FINAL n={len(result)} internal_obj={best_o:.6f} evaluated={val if np.isfinite(val) else np.inf}")
    return result
```
